## Recovery and write path (second review, 2026-11-06)

| Managed | C++ | Status |
|---|---|---|
| `Db/DbImpl.Open.cs` (`RecoverLogFilesAsync`, `ProcessLogFilesAsync`) | `DBImpl::ProcessLogFile` (`flushed`), `MaybeFlushFinalMemtableOrRestoreActiveLogFiles` | **fixed (P1).** With avoid_flush_during_recovery, a memtable that filled up during the replay was flushed, but the rest stayed in the memtable, so the column family's log number could not advance and the next open replayed the WALs again on top of the flushed data (merge operands applied twice: 5828 instead of 3000). Like upstream, a flush during recovery now makes recovery flush every column family at the end and advance its log number past the replayed WALs. Tests: `DbBasicTests.AvoidFlushDuringRecoveryWithMidRecoveryFlushDoesNotReplayTwice`; compat `RecoveryWritePathCompatTests.AvoidFlushDuringRecoveryWithFullMemtableIsNotReplayedTwice` (each engine recovers twice, then the other opens). |
| `Db/DbImpl.Write.cs` (`ThrottleLowPriWritesAsync`), `Db/Compaction/WriteController.cs` (`LowPriRateLimiter`) | `DBImpl::ThrottleLowPriWritesIfNeeded`, `WriteController::low_pri_rate_limiter_`, `RecalculateWriteStallConditions` (delayed_write_rate / 4) | **ported.** WriteOptions.LowPri was ignored. While the write controller needs compaction to speed up (stop, delay or compaction pressure), a low_pri write with no_slowdown fails with Incomplete "Low priority write stall"; otherwise it is charged to a 1 MiB/s generic rate limiter (IO_HIGH, in chunks of at most the burst size; async writes wait asynchronously). With 2PC, batches with a commit or rollback marker are exempt. When a delay ends the limiter's rate becomes 1/4 of the delayed write rate. Like upstream, the condition is read outside the DB mutex. The limiter is created on first use. Test: `DbBasicTests.LowPriWritesAreThrottledUnderCompactionPressure`. Not ported: the PERF write_delay_time timer around the wait. |
| `Options/ColumnFamilyOptions.cs` (`DisallowMemtableWrites`), `Db/DbImpl.Write.cs` (`CfForInsert`, `ValidateColumnFamilies`), `Db/DbImpl.Open.cs` | `advanced_options.h` disallow_memtable_writes, `MemTableInserter::SeekToColumnFamily`, `WriteBatchInternal::GetColumnFamilyIdAndTimestampSize`, `DBImpl::ValidateOptions` | **ported.** OPTIONS name `disallow_memtable_writes` (immutable). Writes to such a column family fail with InvalidArgument "This column family has disallow_memtable_writes=true"; WAL replay into one fails the open with the same error; the default column family cannot set it. Deviation: upstream rejects at WriteBatch::Put(handle); the managed WriteBatch records column family ids only, so the write path checks the batch before the WAL (only once some column family sets the option, one volatile read otherwise) and the whole batch is rejected; SeekToColumnFamily's check stays as the backstop. Not ported: SanitizeOptions' switch to the vector memtable (MarkReadOnly enforcement). Tests: `DbBasicTests.DisallowMemtableWrites` (port of DBBasicTest.DisallowMemtableWrite); compat `RecoveryWritePathCompatTests.DisallowMemtableWritesAgreesWithNative` (OPTIONS both ways, identical messages). |
| `Db/WriteThread.cs` (`EnterAsBatchGroupLeader`) | `WriteThread::EnterAsBatchGroupLeader` (r_list) | **fixed (throughput).** The group stopped at the first incompatible writer. Incompatible writers now stay queued in order and the compatible ones behind them join (upstream's r_list grafted after the group). Criteria now include protection_bytes_per_key and the growth limit is max_write_batch_group_size_bytes / 8 (was a fixed 128 KiB, equal for the default 1 MiB). rate_limiter_priority (WAL writes are not rate limited) and WBWI ingestion are not ported, so those criteria never apply. Test: `DbColumnFamilyAndConcurrencyTests.WriteGroupTakesCompatibleWritersBehindIncompatibleOnes`. |
| `Db/DbImpl.Write.cs` (`InsertGroupSerially`, `InsertWritersSerially`, `FinishParallelGroup`, `LeadMemTableGroup`, `HandleMemTableInsertFailure`) | `DBImpl::WriteImpl` / `PipelinedWriteImpl` ("Don't publish a partial batch write"), `HandleMemTableInsertFailure`, `WriteBatchInternal::InsertInto(write_group)` | **fixed.** A failed memtable insert used to be reported to its writer only: the group was still published and the DB kept accepting writes. Now the serial insert stops at the first failure (InsertInto returns it; the leader reports it too), the group's sequences are not published, and a fatal background error with reason kMemTable stops further writes; a reopen recovers the batch from the WAL. Deviation (stricter): upstream checks only the status of the writer that finishes a parallel group last (a TODO there); the port checks every writer of the group. unordered_write publishes before its inserts and, like upstream, does not call HandleMemTableInsertFailure. Deviation: once a column family was dropped, batches written without column family validation (writes through a dropped `ColumnFamily` handle) are checked before the WAL and fail with InvalidArgument "Invalid column family specified in write batch"; upstream lets them reach the memtable insert (SeekToColumnFamily), where the failure is a fatal background error. A drop that races with a write still ends that way. Not ported: SetAttemptTruncateSize of the failed group's WAL record. Test: `KvProtectionTests.ParallelMemtableInsertDetectsCorruptedEntry` (now asserts the background error, the unpublished group and recovery after reopen). |

## Second review: DB API, properties, EVENT_LOG, PerfContext, Raft

Tests: `tests/RocksDb.Managed.Tests/Db/DestroyAndShutdownTests.cs`, `Features/PerfContextTests.cs`,
`Features/PropertiesAndStatisticsTests.cs`, `tests/RocksDb.Managed.CompatTests/Api/{Destroy,EventLog,
PropertiesStatistics}CompatTests.cs`, `tests/RocksDb.Managed.Replication.Tests/BuildingBlockTests.cs`.

| Finding | C++ | Fix |
|---|---|---|
| `rocksdb.estimate-num-keys` clamped the memtable and table parts separately (native 50, managed 100 after deleting half the flushed keys) | `InternalStats::HandleEstimateNumKeys`, `VersionStorageInfo::GetEstimatedActiveKeys` | **fixed.** One sum (memtables + immutable memtables + estimated active table keys) minus twice the memtable deletes, clamped once; the table part uses the files whose entry stats are loaded (no table reads) and scales by files / samples. Compat: three LSM shapes give native's values. |
| No DestroyDB | `DestroyDB` (db_impl.cc), `DeleteUnaccountedDBFile` / `DeleteExistingTrashDBFile` (file_util.cc) | **ported** (`RocksDatabase.Destroy` / `DestroyAsync`, `Db/DestroyDb.cs`). LOCK first (an open DB fails with IOError and nothing is deleted), every parsed file of the DB directory (METADB-N recursively), trash, db_paths tables / blobs, the archive and a separate wal_dir, through the SstFileManager when set; directories removed in std::set order like upstream, so a DB (or WAL) directory whose `archive` existed stays behind empty, as with native. A missing directory succeeds (upstream's info logger creates it). Compat: native- and managed-created DBs destroyed by either side leave the same files. Deviations: one SstFileManager trash queue instead of a per-call trash bucket (waits for all of the manager's trash); cf_paths not searched (not ported). |
| No public CancelAllBackgroundWork, no OnDBShutdownBegin | `DBImpl::CancelAllBackgroundWork`, `NotifyOnDBShutdownBegin`, `EventListener::OnDBShutdownBegin` | **ported** (`RocksDatabase.CancelAllBackgroundWork(wait)` / async, `EventListener.OnDBShutdownBegin`). Shutdown flush unless already shutting down, CompactionService.CancelAwaitingJobs, OnDBShutdownBegin once per instance (also from Close and from a failed open, with a null DB there), then the shutdown state; Close after it does not flush again. Tests: listener_test.cc's OnDBShutdownBeginOnceForCancelAndClose / OnFailedOpen. |
| Missing properties | `ppt_name_to_info` (internal_stats.cc) | **fixed.** Every name of ppt_name_to_info now answers: compression-ratio-at-level&lt;N&gt; (GetEstimatedCompressionRatioAtLevel), cf-write-stall-stats / db-write-stall-stats (+ map forms; also the "Write Stall (count)" line and map entries of cfstats / dbstats, counted at each RecalculateWriteStallConditions and at WriteBufferManager stalls), block-cache-entry-stats (now the CacheEntryRoleStats text / map) and fast-block-cache-entry-stats (CacheEntryStatsCollector's 10 s / 180 s reuse), num-immutable-mem-table-flushed, num-running-remote-compactions, num-running-bottom-compactions, num-running-compaction-sorted-runs (L0 files + other levels of the registered non-trivial compactions; upstream counts the CompactionMergingIterator children), compaction-abort-count (0: AbortAllCompactions not ported), num-unscheduled-compactions (queued column families), is-file-deletions-enabled, min-log-number-to-keep, min-obsolete-sst-number-to-keep, obsolete-sst-files-size, estimate-oldest-key-time (FIFO without allow_compaction only), live-sst-files-size-at-temperature&lt;N&gt;. num-files-at-level&lt;N&gt; with N &gt;= num_levels is null (was "0"); total-sst-files-size counts the distinct files of every live version (was the current one); the dbstats map uses upstream's keys (db.wal_bytes_written, write-buffer-manager-limit-stops, db.uptime; was db.wal_bytes). Flushes now record min_log_number_to_keep also with a single column family (GetDBRecoveryEditForObsoletingMemTables), as native's MANIFEST does. Compat: the deterministic values equal native's on the same workload; the stall counts are non-zero on both sides. Deviations: the cache entry stats text has seed 0 and table_size 0 and occupancy = entry count (the managed caches expose no hash table); remote-compaction-pool-threads is not a property in 11.12 and not ported. |
| EVENT_LOG records missing | event_helpers.cc, db_impl_open.cc, db_impl_compaction_flush.cc, external_sst_file_ingestion_job.cc, flush_job.cc, compaction_job.cc | **fixed.** table_file_deletion, blob_file_creation, blob_file_deletion, trivial_move, recovery_started, recovery_finished / recovery_failed and ingest_finished are written, and flush_started (total_num_input_entries, num_range_deletes, the real flush_reason string), flush_finished (blob_file_head / tail, immutable_memtables), table_file_creation (checksum, sequence range, the table_properties object, oldest_blob_file_number, status), compaction_started (every input level, oldest_snapshot_seqno) and compaction_finished (cpu micros, blob outputs, num_subcompactions, output_compression, single-delete counters, blob head / tail) now carry upstream's keys in upstream's order; trivial moves log no compaction_finished. Events logged while opening are written after the LOG header. Compat: same workload, same keys in the same order for all 12 event types (and the TableProperties keys of table_file_creation). Deviations: job ids of file deletions are 0; checksums are hex in blob_file_creation; table_file_creation leaves out user-collected properties; seqno_to_time_mapping is "N/A" or "(mapping)" instead of the human-readable mapping; num_subcompactions is always 1. |
| No PerfContext / IOStatsContext | perf_context.h / .cc, perf_context_imp.h, perf_step_timer.h, perf_level.cc, iostats_context.h / .cc | **ported** (`PerfContext`, `PerfMetric` with upstream's 120 metrics generated from DEF_PERF_CONTEXT_METRICS, `PerfLevel`, `IOStatsContext`, `IOStatsMetric`). Thread-local contexts and level, Reset, ToString(exclude_zero_counters) in upstream's format. Updated metrics: user_key_comparison_count (InternalKeyComparator user-key compares), get_from_memtable_count / time, get_from_output_files_time, block_cache_hit_count, block_read_count / byte / time (async reads: count and bytes only), bloom_sst_hit / miss_count (whole-key full filters), iter_seek / next / prev_count, seek_child_seek_count, seek_on / next_on / prev_on_memtable_count, seek_internal_seek_time, internal_key_skipped_count, internal_delete_skipped_count, internal_recent_skipped_count (forward iteration), write_wal_time, write_memtable_time (serial memtable writes); IOStats bytes_read (sync and page-cache-hit reads of LocalFileSystem), bytes_written, fsync_nanos. Zero-cost when disabled: every point reads one static counter of enabled threads; see docs/perf.md for the before/after numbers. Deviations: default level Disable (upstream kEnableCount); IOStats bytes counted only while the thread's level is at least EnableCount (upstream: always); no per-level contexts; mutex, condition, CPU and env timers stay 0; other metrics not updated stay 0. |
| Raft follower commit index used `min(leaderCommit, myLatest)` | Raft §5.3 (AppendEntries receiver rule 5) | **fixed.** `min(leaderCommit, prevLogSeq)`: a heartbeat carries no entries, so only the prefix ending at prevLogSeq is known to match the leader's log; a deposed leader's divergent tail is no longer reported committed. Test: a follower with a term-2 tail past the leader's prevLogSeq commits only up to prevLogSeq and resyncs on a term mismatch inside the tail. |

## TTL merges, merge failure scope, FullMergeV3, transaction APIs

Second correctness review, TTL / merge / transaction items. C++ reference: `main` (11.14); native: the 11.12 package.

| Managed file(s) | C++ source | Status | Tests |
|---|---|---|---|
| `Db/DbWithTtl.cs` (`TtlMergeOperator`) | `utilities/ttl/db_ttl_impl.{h,cc}` (TtlMergeOperator::FullMergeV2 / PartialMergeMulti, RegisterTtlObjects, ttl_merge_op_type_info) | **fixed (P2: merges read the 4-byte time suffix; a native TTL DB with merges was misread).** SanitizeOptions now wraps the column family's merge operator. The suffix is removed from the base value and every operand (a zero-copy operand view, `MergeOperands.WithoutSuffix`) before the user operator, its failure scope is passed through, and the current time is appended to full and partial merge results. A base value or operand shorter than 4 bytes fails the merge (upstream logs and returns false), which surfaces as Corruption / MergeOperatorFailed. OPTIONS: `{id=TtlMergeOperator;user_operator=<id>;}`, byte-identical to native, and `MergeOperator.CreateBuiltin` recreates it. Upstream records no statistics in TtlMergeOperator (NUMBER_MERGE_FAILURES is MergeHelper's, which the port does not record). | `TtlDatabaseTests` (StringAppend and UInt64Add through memtable, flush, compaction and reopen; merge results stamped with the merge time; short operand / base value; partial merge); compat `MergeOperatorCompatTests.TtlMergesMatchNativeDbWithTtl` (stringappend, uint64add): native DBWithTTL writes timestamped puts / merges / deletes (SST + WAL), managed reads the same values, adds merges, flushes and compacts, native reads managed's results and compacts; both OPTIONS files name the operator identically |
| `Db/DbWithTtl.cs` (`TtlDatabase`, `TtlColumnFamily`, `TtlCompactionFilterFactory`) | `DBWithTTL::Open` (descriptors + ttls, read_only), `DBWithTTLImpl::SanitizeOptions`, `CreateColumnFamilyWithTtl`, `CreateColumnFamily`, `SetTtl`, `GetTtl`, `Merge`, `Write` | **ported.** Per column family TTLs (InvalidArgument when the counts differ), OpenReadOnly, CreateColumnFamilyWithTtl (CreateColumnFamily = TTL 0), Merge / Put / Delete / Get / iterators per column family. SanitizeOptions like upstream: a user `CompactionFilter` is wrapped by a TtlCompactionFilter (then there is no TTL factory: GetTtl is InvalidArgument and SetTtl is a no-op, as upstream); otherwise a TtlCompactionFilterFactory wraps the user `CompactionFilterFactory` (which the port used to drop). Deviations: a default column family not listed at open is given the TTL machinery with TTL 0 (upstream's DB::Open adds it with unsanitized options); SetTtl also applies to compactions already running that use the shared TTL filter. | `TtlDatabaseTests.ColumnFamiliesHaveTheirOwnTtl`, `UserCompactionFilterAndFactoryAreWrapped` |
| `Db/MergeOperator.cs`, `Db/MergeHelper.cs`, `GetContext.cs`, `Iterators/DbIter.cs` | `db/merge_operator.cc` (FullMergeV3), `include/rocksdb/merge_operator.h` (OpFailureScope, MergeOperationInputV3 / OutputV3), `db/merge_helper.cc` (TimedFullMergeCommonImpl, the TimedFullMergeImpl visitors, MergeUntil) | **ported.** `FullMerge(..., out MergeOperationFailureScope)` (FullMergeV2's op_failure_scope) and `FullMergeV3(in MergeOperationInputV3, ref MergeOperationOutputV3)`: the base value is none / plain / wide columns and the result a plain value (`NewValue`) or columns (`AddColumn`, sorted afterwards); the default implementation is upstream's (V2 directly, or on the default column keeping the other columns). Every merge (Get, MultiGet, iterators, WBWI, flush and compaction) goes through FullMergeV3. Value-only callers take the default column of a wide result; GetEntity, iterators and MergeUntil keep the entity (kTypeWideColumnEntity output). kDefault reads as kTryMerge; in MergeUntil a kMustMerge failure returns MergeInProgress with the operands kept (with a base value, the iterator is left on it so it is output after them); other failures throw Corruption / MergeOperatorFailed. Reads fail on every scope, as upstream. Deviations: upstream's third result form (an existing operand by reference) is not offered; the 4 GB result check is not ported. The WBWI read paths still merge an entity base on its default column (equivalent to the default FullMergeV3; an operator overriding FullMergeV3 sees a plain base there). | `MergeOperatorV3Tests` (port of DBMergeOperatorTest.MergeOperatorFailsWithMustMerge: three cases x three positions, before flush, after flush and after compaction, iterator directions, GetMergeOperands; kDefault / kTryMerge fail CompactRange; a V3 operator turning plain bases into entities and updating entity bases through memtable, flush and compaction; the default V3 on entities with and without a default column) |
| `Utilities/Transactions/Transaction.cs`, `PessimisticTransaction.cs`, `OptimisticTransactionDatabase.cs`, `WriteUnpreparedTxn.cs`, `IndexedWriteBatch.cs`, `LockTracker.cs`, WBWI | `transaction_base.cc` (MultiGet, MultiGetForUpdate, Put/Merge/Delete/SingleDelete with assume_tracked, GetNumPuts/PutEntities/Deletes/Merges/Keys, GetElapsedTime, DisableIndexing / GetBatchForWrite, SavePoint counters, Reinitialize), `pessimistic_transaction.cc` (TryLock assume_tracked, SetLockTimeout), `optimistic_transaction.cc` (TryLock) | **ported.** MultiGet reads through WBWI MultiGetFromBatchAndDB with the transaction's DB reader (WriteUnprepared: one read callback for all keys); MultiGetForUpdate locks / tracks every key first and fails as a whole. assume_tracked writes skip validation and re-tracking; a pessimistic transaction rejects an untracked key with InvalidArgument "assume_tracked is set but it is not tracked yet" (deviation: the port releases the lock it took for that write; upstream keeps it without tracking it, so it is never released); optimistic transactions ignore it (upstream asserts). Counters are restored by RollbackToSavePoint and reset by Clear / Reinitialize; ElapsedTime is a TimeSpan. DisableIndexing appends without indexing (and without sub-batch counting, like upstream's plain WriteBatch); a rebuild of the index (RollbackToSavePoint) indexes everything. upstream's GetForUpdate has no assume_tracked (do_validate exists already). | `TransactionTests` on WriteCommitted, WritePrepared and WriteUnprepared: ports of AssumeExclusiveTracked, MultiGetBatchedTest, DisableIndexingTest (not WriteUnprepared, as upstream), the SavepointTest counters and the TimeoutTest SetLockTimeout part; `AssumeTrackedOnUntrackedKeyIsInvalidArgument`, `MultiGetForUpdateLocksAllKeys`; `OptimisticTransactionTests.MultiGetForUpdateTracksKeys` |
| Transaction and WBWI reads over a DB merge chain | `WriteBatchWithIndex::GetFromBatchAndDB` (MergeAcrossBatchAndDB), `BaseDeltaIterator` | verified: batch operands over a DB value that is itself an unresolved chain (Put in one SST, operands in a second SST and in the memtable, and a chain without a base value) give the same values through GetFromBatchAndDB, MultiGetFromBatchAndDB, GetEntityFromBatchAndDB, the base-delta iterator in both directions, also at a snapshot inside the chain, and transaction Get / GetForUpdate / MultiGet / iterator on all write policies. | `WriteBatchWithIndexTests.BatchMergesOverDbMergeChainAcrossMemtableAndFiles`, `TransactionTests.BatchMergesOverDbMergeChainAcrossMemtableAndFiles` |

Observation (not a TTL bug, flush is owned elsewhere): the port's flush does not run the merge helper (FlushJob keeps
Put + operands; see FlushJob), so in a TTL database a merge result is stamped at the first compaction instead of at the
flush, and expires correspondingly later than with native.

Performance (BenchmarkDotNet, net10.0, `MergeBenchmarks.cs` / `ScanBenchmarks.cs`, a shared 4-core container with load
average 11-23 from other jobs, so absolute numbers are noisy). The default merge path detects (once per operator type)
that neither FullMergeV3 nor the failure-scope overload is overridden and calls FullMerge directly: `FullMergeBenchmarks`
measures MergeHelper.TimedFullMerge at the same cost as the bare virtual FullMerge call (UInt64Add with base 27.15 vs
27.41 ns, StringAppend 44.5 vs 46.6 ns, both "-" allocated). Before / after the change: `MergeCompactionBenchmarks`
(bottommost CompactRange of 50,000 keys x Put + 4 UInt64Add operands over 5 L0 files) median 42.2 ms / 38.4 ms (same
816 KB allocated per compaction); `MergeScanBenchmarks` (5 operands per key over 4 L0 files + memtable) 9.54 / 8.29 ms
(24-byte operands) and 50.4 / 52.0 ms (1 KiB), "-" allocated. A first version that went through the FullMergeV3 adapter
for every merge cost about 30 ns more per merge, hence the fast path.

## Combinatorial round trips (2026-11-06)

Findings of the pairwise option matrix, the crash matrix and the 2PC round trips in `tests/RocksDb.Managed.RoundTrip`
(see [testing.md](testing.md), "The option matrix"). C++ reference: `main` (11.14); native: the 11.12 package.

| Finding | C++ source | Status | Tests |
|---|---|---|---|
| Get / MultiGet looked at one file per sorted level | `FilePicker::GetNextFile` (db/version_set.cc): `if (curr_level_ > 0 && cmp_largest < 0)` ends the level, so a key equal to a file's largest user key continues with the next file; `FilePickerMultiGet` likewise | **fixed (P1: wrong read results).** The versions of one user key can span adjacent files of a level (compaction outputs are cut at the target size by internal key, upstream as well). When the lookup was not done in the first file (merge operands whose base value is in the next file, or only versions newer than the snapshot), the managed Get, GetAsync (also with timestamps), MultiGet and MultiGetAsync went on to the next level: a merge chain lost its base value (Get returned just the operands while iterators were right) or an older snapshot missed its value. The matrix found it on a DB with snapshots, 4-subcompaction compactions and 1 KiB blocks (native read the same files correctly). | `DbMultiGetTests.KeyVersionsSpanningFilesOfALevel` (fails without the fix); `MatrixRoundTripTests` |
| `TransactionDatabase.Write` locked and wrote every record of the batch | `PessimisticTransaction::LockBatch` (utilities/transactions/pessimistic_transaction.cc): its handler overrides PutCF, PutEntityCF, MergeCF and DeleteCF only; the WriteBatch::Handler defaults (include/rocksdb/write_batch.h) return InvalidArgument | **fixed.** A SingleDelete on a non-default column family ("non-default column family and SingleDeleteCF not implemented"), a DeleteRange ("DeleteRangeCF not implemented"), a TimedPut and a blob index now fail the write with InvalidArgument before anything is locked or written; a SingleDelete on the default column family is accepted without locking the key, as upstream. | `TransactionTests.WriteBatchWithUnsupportedRecordsIsRejected` (all write policies); `TransactionRoundTripTests` |
| unordered_write with manual_wal_flush hangs native | `DBImpl::ConcurrentWriteGroupToWAL` holds `wal_write_mutex_` and calls `WriteToWAL`, which locks it again when `manual_wal_flush_ && !two_write_queues_` (db/db_impl/db_impl_write.cc; the "two queues: already held" assumption misses unordered_write without two_write_queues). Same code on main | **native bug (11.12 and main).** The first write self-deadlocks (stack: rocksdb_write, WriteImpl, WriteImplWALOnly, ConcurrentWriteGroupToWAL, WriteToWAL, Mutex::Lock). The combination is excluded from both matrices. | `MatrixRoundTripTests.Excluded`, `CrashMatrixRoundTripTests.Excluded` |
| Native iterators return freed memory for blob values merged with the max operator | `DBIter::MergeWithBlobBaseValue` (db/db_iter.cc) calls `blob_state_.Reset()` after `MergeWithPlainBaseValue`; when the operator's result is the base value itself (MaxOperator sets `existing_operand`), `MergeHelper::TimedFullMerge` returns it as `pinned_value_`, which then points into the released blob buffer. Same code on main | **native bug (11.12 and main).** Native scans returned values whose first 32 bytes were heap garbage; native point lookups and the managed iterator (which copies the merge result) return the right value. The matrix gives no blob files to column families with the max operator. | `MatrixRoundTripTests.ApplyCf` |
| Hash index and hash skip list with keys outside the prefix extractor's domain | `HashIndexBuilder::AddIndexEntry` and `BlockPrefixIndex::GetBlocks` (table/block_based/index_builder.h, block_prefix_index.cc) and `HashSkipListRep` call `Transform` without `InDomain`; a fixed prefix longer than the key reads past it | **outside the contract (undefined behaviour upstream).** Native 11.12 then misses such keys (Get NotFound for the empty key and 1- to 3-byte keys with a 4-byte fixed prefix); the managed port finds them. The matrix uses the hash index and the hash skip list only with the capped prefix extractor (every key in domain). | `MatrixRoundTripTests.AllKeysInDomain` |
| inplace_update_support with range deletions | `MemTable::Update` (db/memtable.cc): an in-place update keeps the overwritten entry's sequence number ("existing_seq prevails") | **upstream semantics, matched by the port.** A range tombstone written between the two puts deletes the new value in both engines. inplace_update_support also breaks snapshot reads. The matrix uses neither range deletions nor snapshots with in-place updates. | `MatrixRoundTripTests.Build` |
| ingest_behind and allow_global_seqno=false placement rules | `ExternalSstFileIngestionJob::CheckLevelForIngestedBehindFile` (refuses while files with sequence number 0 are above the last level), `AssignLevelAndSeqnoForIngestedFile` (FIFO always ingests into L0 with a global seqno; open snapshots force one) | **verified: both engines agree**, including the "Global seqno is required, but disabled" failures. The generator ingests behind only fresh keys without an open snapshot and does not mix the two modes. | `MatrixRoundTripTests` (ingest levels) |
| disableWAL write restrictions | `DBImpl::WriteImpl` (sync writes need the WAL; disableWAL with recycle_log_file_num &gt; 0 is InvalidArgument) | **verified: both engines agree.** | `MatrixRoundTripTests` (excluded pair; disableWAL writes are never synced) |
| change_level followed by ingest_behind | `DBImpl::ReFitLevel` moves the last level's sequence-number-0 files (ingested behind) up; `CheckLevelForIngestedBehindFile` then refuses until a compaction moves them back | **timing-dependent in both engines** (verified with a direct script: both move the files back to the last level shortly after). Excluded from the matrix. | `MatrixRoundTripTests.Excluded` |
| TransactionDB with unordered_write | `PessimisticTransactionDB` open checks ("WRITE_COMMITTED is incompatible with unordered_writes") | not exercised (excluded from the crash matrix). | |

Not covered by the round trips: wide columns (the 11.12 C API has no `PutEntity`), `preclude_last_level_data_seconds`
(no C API setter), separated key/value data blocks (`separate_key_value_in_data_block` has no C API setter, and setting
it through `rocksdb_get_options_from_string` would replace the table factory the other setters configure),
`db_paths` / `cf_paths` and `last_level_temperature` (C API setters exist; not crossed in this pass: on one file system
they only move files or tag them), user-defined timestamps inside the matrix (covered by `FeatureRoundTripTests` only,
since UDT excludes range deletions and ingestion) and OptimisticTransactionDB (no prepared state to recover). The managed port has
no multi-column-family `Flush(FlushOptions, IReadOnlyList<ColumnFamily>)` overload, so the managed side of a FlushAll
flushes the column families one after the other (native uses `rocksdb_flush_cfs`).

## Audit #4: full correctness pass against the C++ sources (2026-11-10 / 11)

A fourth pass over the whole library, split into 17 slices plus follow-up passes on the suspicions and on the files a
slice did not reach. Every finding below was proven by a test that failed before the fix; native comparisons use the
11.12 package. Tests: `tests/RocksDb.Managed.Tests/Validation/Audit4*Tests.cs` (unit) and
`tests/RocksDb.Managed.CompatTests/Audit4/Audit4*CompatTests.cs` (differential against native), one file per slice.
`RocksDb.Managed.Replication` (a port of rocksdb-sharp, not of the C++ tree) was not part of the audit.

### Fixed

| Area | C++ source | What was wrong (now fixed) |
|---|---|---|
| Compaction job | `CompactionJob::Prepare` (preserve-time floor), `compaction_service_job.cc` | A kBottommostFiles compaction ignored the version's preserve-time min seqno: nothing was zeroed and the output was re-marked and rewritten forever (local and remote). Dropped range tombstones were counted for the last output only. |
| Compaction job, flush | `builder.cc` (BuildTable), `CompactionOutputs::AddRangeDels`, `VersionSet::ApproximateSize` | `compensated_range_deletion_size` was never computed (flush and non-bottommost compaction outputs; feeds compaction scoring). |
| Background compaction | `ProcessManifestWrites` file quarantine, `BackgroundCompaction` (kManifestWrite) | Compaction outputs were deleted after a failed MANIFEST write although the record may have reached the MANIFEST: the next open failed on a missing file. The failure is now reported with reason ManifestWrite. |
| CompactRange | `CompactRangeInternal` (MaybeAddTimestampsToRange, RangesOverlapWithMemtables) | Bounds on a column family with user-defined timestamps were used with their last 8 bytes read as a timestamp. With both bounds, the memtable is flushed only when it overlaps the range. A canceled DisableManualCompaction left manual compactions paused. |
| Compaction pickers | `GenerateLevel0NonOverlapping`, `Compaction::IsTrivialMove`, `SortFileByRoundRobin`, `FileTtlBooster`, `compaction_picker_level.cc` (db_paths) | L0 non-overlap compared user keys (native trivially moves files sharing only a boundary user key); universal sorted-run compactions were trivially moved without allow_trivial_move; kRoundRobin ordered L0 by key; no TTL boost in kMinOverlappingRatio; multi-file trivial moves with several db_paths; compaction outputs ignored `Compaction::output_compression` (CompactFiles compression, FIFO intra-L0). |
| DBIter | `db_iter.cc` (FindValueForCurrentKey, FindValueForCurrentKeyUsingSeek, FindUserKeyBeforeSavedKey) | Reverse movement with iter_start_ts over range tombstones returned other positions than native. DbIter and the multi-CF iterators stayed valid after an inner iterator failed. NewMultiScan accepted overlapping / unordered ranges. Async moves recorded no tickers. |
| Point lookups | `MemTable::Get`, `TableCache::Get`, `DBImpl::GetImpl`, `MultiGetCommon`, `DBImpl::KeyMayExist`, `GetContext::SaveValue` | `ignore_range_deletions` was ignored by Get / MultiGet; a read timestamp on a CF without timestamps was accepted by GetAsync / MultiGet; KeyMayExist under a memtable range tombstone and with a mismatched timestamp; the timestamp reported when the newest entry carries kMaxTs; the memtable entry checksum is verified before the visibility check. |
| MultiGet | `DBImpl::MultiGetImpl` (MAX_BATCH_SIZE), `hash_index_reader.cc` | `value_size_soft_limit` is applied per batch of 32 sorted keys. A kHashSearch table whose prefixes block cannot be read fails to open. |
| Level iterator | `LevelIterator::Seek` | The prefix check after a skipped file passed keys with the user-defined timestamp to the prefix extractor (a key could be skipped). |
| Table reader | `full_filter_block.cc`, `partitioned_filter_block.cc`, `meta_blocks.cc` (GetGlobalSequenceNumber), `sst_file_reader` | An unreadable filter block failed the open / lookup (upstream ignores it); the same for a partitioned filter's top-level index, and for a filter partition when there is no block cache; external file version > 2 was Corruption; `SstFileReader.MultiGet` ignored the snapshot. |
| Blocks | `block.cc` (raw_key_ as delta base), `filter_policy.cc` | With a global sequence number BlockIter patched the footer in the buffer that is the delta base of the next entry: a following key sharing those bytes got the seqno spliced into its user key (ingested files). Internal keys shorter than their footer and a FastLocalBloom shorter than a cache line no longer throw non-RocksDb exceptions. RibbonFilterPolicy honors the compaction style (FIFO / none are "bottommost"). |
| Table builder, SstFileWriter | `block_based_table_factory.cc` (InitializeOptions), `sst_file_writer.cc`, `block_based_table_builder.cc` | format_version < 2 is raised to 2 (and persisted so); SstFileWriter wrote a creation time, no original file number and a new session id per Open, and left the file behind when Finish failed; uncompressed data blocks are counted as "bypassed". |
| Compression | `util/compression.cc` | A zlib stream cut before its end marker and an LZ4 block without payload are Corruption. |
| Memtable | `memtable.cc` (SaveValue under the in-place lock), `memtable.h` (UpdateWriteBufferSize) | A TimedPut value was returned without the read lock under inplace_update_support; SetOptions could grow a live memtable that has a bloom filter. |
| Range tombstones | `range_del_aggregator.cc` (TruncatedRangeDelIterator) | The truncated largest bound treated a seqno-0 deletion as covered (unit level; no reachable file layout found). |
| Write path | `WriteThread::BeginWriteStall`, `DBImpl::WALIOStatusCheck`, `WritableFileWriter::seen_error_`, `SetAttemptTruncateSize`, `WriteBatch::ComputeContentFlags` | NoSlowdown writers queued behind a stalled leader waited instead of failing with Incomplete; a failed WAL write always became a background error (only with paranoid_checks upstream); the WAL writer had no sticky error (a retried sync could report success); the WAL is cut back at close after a failed memtable insert; a malformed raw batch in a parallel group failed the group after the WAL write without a background error. |
| Recovery | `DBImpl::ProcessLogRecord` / `InitializeWriteBatchForLogRecord`, `RecoverEpochNumbers`, `MaybeUpdateNextFileNumber`, `DBImpl::Recover` | A WAL record with a malformed batch fails the open in every mode (as native); a batch the memtable refuses is replayed up to the refused entry; sequences above 56 bits are corruption; MANIFESTs without epoch numbers open; next_file_number skips files found on disk; a new DB over a stray WAL is Corruption; open moves files down for level_compaction_dynamic_level_bytes; read-only open statuses (error_if_wal_file_exists, missing DB); obsolete IDENTITY file; full_history_ts_low never goes down in replay; a corrupt atomic group size is Corruption. |
| Versions | `VersionBuilder::ApplyFileAddition` | A live edit adding a table file already in the LSM tree is rejected before it reaches the MANIFEST. |
| Error handler | `ErrorHandler::SetBGError`, `CheckAndSetRecoveryAndBGError` | A listener clearing the status no longer suppresses data-loss, manual_wal_flush WAL and retryable I/O errors; the recovery context follows the error that becomes the background error. (Listeners still replace or suppress errors handled by HandleKnownErrors.) |
| RepairDB | `Repairer::Run`, `ConvertLogToTable` | All WALs were replayed as the newest data, so a stale WAL could shadow newer table data. |
| Secondary | `DBImplSecondary::RecoverLogFiles` | Catch-up dropped a corrupt WAL record silently and applied the later ones. |
| Ingestion, import, checkpoint | `external_sst_file_ingestion_job.cc`, `import_column_family_job.cc`, `checkpoint_impl.cc`, `db_filesnapshot.cc` | Any range tombstone counted as an overlap (wrong level / seqno / refusal); a path listed twice was rejected; move_files kept the original after a copy fallback; import ordered L0 by the exported epochs; stale staging directories; log_size_for_flush with allow_2pc; a checkpoint / backup without a flush while blob direct write files are in flight is NotSupported. |
| Transactions | `transaction_base.cc`, `point_lock_tracker.cc`, `pessimistic_transaction_db.cc`, `point_lock_manager.cc` | SetSnapshotOnNextOperation fired on reads; UndoGetForUpdate bookkeeping released a lock early; direct DB writes skipped locking with skip_concurrency_control; timestamped column families are verified at open and at creation; a reused transaction kept its max write batch size; a pending snapshot request at commit needs a commit timestamp; a no-wait lock timeout records the blockers; locking in a dropped column family is InvalidArgument. |
| WritePrepared / WriteUnprepared | `write_unprepared_txn.cc`, `write_prepared_txn.cc`, `util/duplicate_detector.h`, `write_prepared_txn_db.h` (SubBatchCounter) | RollbackToSavePoint over a flushed save point did not restore untracked keys; an iterator after a flushed unprepared batch with two write queues; rollback and recovery de-duplicate keys with the column family comparator (a recovered prepared sequence could read as committed); a DeleteRange begin key is not a duplicate of a point key; GetUpdatesSince is NotSupported with seq_per_batch. |
| Options | `column_family.cc` (SanitizeCfOptions), `DBImpl::ValidateOptions`, `BlockBasedTableFactory::ValidateOptions` / `InitializeOptions`, `util/string_util.cc`, `advanced_options.h` | Universal compaction with a non-block-based table could not open; DB and table option validation at open; table options sanitized before they are persisted; atomic_flush sanitizes min_write_buffer_number_to_merge; numeric and double value syntax; deprecated mutable options accepted by SetOptions; `DbOptions.Clone` dropped a custom FileChecksumGenFactory; prepopulate_block_cache=kFlushAndCompaction; `max_compaction_bytes` defaults to target_file_size_base * 25. |
| Cache | `clock_cache.cc` (FinishSlotInsert, CorrectNearOverflow), `block_fetcher.cc`, `secondary_cache_adapter.cc` | HyperClockCache handle inserts started one countdown too high (priorities had no effect); the persistent cache was filled with fill_cache = false; role stats snapshots shared arrays; tiered placeholder charge. |
| Backup, WAL listing | `backup_engine.cc`, `wal_manager.cc` (ReadFirstRecord / ReadFirstLine) | A meta file cut in its last line was accepted; an unreadable table file was backed up under a legacy name; GetBackupInfo status; progress callback; a too-small or corrupt first WAL record dropped the WAL silently; empty compressed WALs; the archive size limit deleted the wrong files. |
| Properties, statistics, logging, listeners | `internal_stats.cc`, `histogram.cc`, `auto_roll_logger.cc`, `db_impl_compaction_flush.cc` | A trailing number in a property name is an argument; histogram text; keep_log_file_num counts the current LOG; aggregated block cache properties count a shared cache once; cfstats CompactedFiles; FlushJobInfo stall flags. `MemoryFileSystem`: a failed rename keeps the source. |
| Listeners, filter perf counters | `builder.cc` / `compaction_job.cc` (LogAndNotifyTableFileCreationFinished), `NotifyOnFlushBegin/Completed`, `NotifyOnCompactionBegin/Completed`, `NotifyOnMemTableSealed`, `FullFilterBlockReader::MayMatch` | OnTableFileCreated is raised when the table is finished, before the install, also for a failed flush output and for the recovery flush; no flush / compaction / memtable-sealed notifications after shutdown began; a manual compaction started while manual compactions are paused raises neither Begin nor Completed; bloom_sst_hit / miss perf counters count prefix probes. Not raised: creation events for failed or empty individual compaction outputs and for remote compaction outputs. |
| PlainTable, CuckooTable, tracing, tools | `plain_table_*.cc`, `cuckoo_table_reader.cc`, `trace_replay.cc`, `replayer_impl.cc`, `ldb_cmd.cc` | PlainTable format_version property; corrupt sizes / geometry / trace record sizes are Corruption; replay ends on an undecodable record; ldb entity columns, `dump --to` in comparator order, `idump --max_keys=0`, map properties; sst_dump TimedPut values. |
| Trie index | `louds_trie.cc` | A data block ending with the empty user key failed the flush for good. The empty separator is stored as the root's prefix key (upstream drops the handle and writes a broken index). |

### Open (tests kept with `[Ignore]`)

- **atomic_flush**: a full memtable switches and flushes only its own column family; upstream switches every column
  family with unflushed data and commits one atomic flush (`DBImpl::ScheduleFlushes`). Only error recovery and manual
  recovery flushes are atomic. `Audit4FollowUpCompactionTests.AtomicFlushFlushesEveryColumnFamilyWhenOneMemtableIsFull`.
- **DBWithTTL and SingleDelete**: native `DBWithTTL::Write` drops a SingleDelete inside a user batch (its handler has no
  SingleDeleteCF); the port applies it. `Audit4FollowUpReadCompatTests.ManagedTtlWriteTreatsSingleDeleteOfABatchLikeNative`.

### Known deviations recorded by this audit

- **Version::Get early stop (upstream bug, not ported).** `Version::Get` leaves the file loop once
  `max_covering_tombstone_seq > 0`; when a compaction cut two versions of one user key into adjacent files of a level
  below an older untruncated range tombstone, native `Get` returns a merge result without its base value (native
  MultiGet and iterators are right: MultiGetFromSST applies the same stop to a by-value copy). The port has no early
  stop. Pinned by `Audit4ReadPathCompatTests.UpstreamGetStopsAtStaleTombstoneWhenUserKeySpansTwoFiles`. Consequence:
  KeyMayExist may answer true where upstream answers false for a key under a table-file range tombstone.
- **kForceOptimized manual compaction.** In `compaction_picker.cc` the loop clearing `covering_the_whole_range` runs
  over an already swapped (empty) vector, so native stops after the first run of old bottommost files; the port
  rewrites the old files after a skipped new one, as the option documents (derived from reading).
- **MultiGet error shape.** A read timestamp on a CF without timestamps and block / index I/O errors fail the whole
  call; upstream sets per-key statuses (InvalidArgument, and Incomplete for the other keys of the call).
- **Commit pre-check failures leave the transaction usable.** After "Must assign a commit timestamp" (or the pending
  snapshot request error) the transaction stays Started with its locks and can be retried; upstream Clear()s it.
  Timestamped snapshots (CreateTimestampedSnapshot, CommitAndTryCreateSnapshot) are not ported: with a commit
  timestamp set, a pending snapshot request is ignored at commit.
- **Merge with a timestamp and no merge operator** is refused up front with NotSupported (upstream fails at the
  memtable with a fatal background error).
- **Empty WAL record.** A group whose callbacks all failed writes no WAL record (upstream writes a header-only one).
- **OnErrorRecoveryBegin veto.** For retryable I/O errors upstream ignores the listener's auto_recovery; the port
  lets it veto the automatic recovery.
- **Lock wait past a holder's expiration.** When the holder has expired but its locks cannot be stolen, upstream
  returns TimedOut at the holder's expiration (FIXME upstream); the built-in lock path keeps waiting until the
  requester's own deadline. The custom-mutex path follows upstream.
- **Trie index holding only the empty user key.** Native finds the key by Get and forward iteration but not by
  SeekToLast / SeekForPrev (its reverse descent never looks at a label-less root's prefix key).
- **Plain-write sub-batch counting** accepts PutEntity / TimedPut / DeleteRange in a WritePrepared DB (upstream's
  SubBatchCounter has no handler for them).
- **Sticky writer errors** exist for the WAL writer only (not for MANIFEST / SST writers).
- **CompactRange on a UDT column family** always flushes the memtable; `ClipColumnFamily` rejects a UDT column family
  up front.
- Not ported, found by this audit: `MergeOperator::ShouldMerge`, `ReadOptions::merge_operand_count_threshold`,
  `SstFileWriter::PutEntity`, `ReadOptions::auto_refresh_iterator_with_snapshot`,
  `FlushOptions::allow_write_stall` / `CompactRangeOptions::allow_write_stall` (accepted, ignored),
  `Compaction::FilterInputsForCompactionIterator`, the option names `use_separate_remote_compaction_pool`,
  `track_options_file_number_in_manifest`, `rate_limiter_bytes_per_sec`, `skip_standard_index`, the
  NUMBER_BLOCK_COMPRESSION_REJECTED / BYPASSED and BYTES_COMPRESSION_* tickers in the table builder, blob byte
  columns of the level stats.

### Coverage of this audit

Read side by side with the C++ sources (first pass or follow-up): compaction iterator and job, MergeUntil, flush
(BuildTable), CompactRange / CompactFiles / ReFitLevel, the pickers and VersionStorageInfo, DBIter and the multi-CF
iterators, Get / MultiGet (DB, version and table level), GetContext, table cache and row cache, level iterator, range
tombstone fragmenter and aggregator, inline skip list, block iterators and index builders, footer, SstFileWriter /
SstFileReader, the memtable, WriteBatch, write thread, write path incl. the WAL-only queue, WAL reader for tailing,
transaction log iterator, VersionEdit, MANIFEST replay, DB open and WAL recovery, the error handler's classification
and SetBGError, obsolete-file deletion (C# side), secondary WAL replay, ingestion, import / export, checkpoint, blob
files, wide-column serialization, pessimistic / optimistic / write-prepared / write-unprepared transactions, point and
range lock managers, WriteBatchWithIndex and BaseDeltaIterator, options parsing / sanitization / validation, the
secondary cache adapter and compressed / tiered secondary caches, backup engine (from line 940), WAL manager,
seqno-to-time mapping, internal stats properties, histograms, rate limiter, file systems, PlainTable, CuckooTable,
trace formats, trie index factory. Option defaults and the ticker / histogram name tables were diffed by script.

Not read against the C++ in this audit (previous audits and the byte-identical compat tests still apply): the filter
builders of `filter_policy.cc`, the Ribbon banding (`ribbon_alg.h` up to the interleaved query; `bloom_impl.h`, the
Ribbon query / configuration and the filter block readers were read), `block_builder.cc`, `meta_blocks.cc` /
`table_properties.cc`, `block_fetcher.cc`; `lru_cache.cc` and the
lookup / release / evict half of `clock_cache.cc`, the persistent cache, cache dump / load; the recovery half of the
error handler (Resume), `version_builder.cc` beyond ApplyFileAddition, `repair.cc` beyond the WAL conversion; the
background flush / compaction scheduling bodies (BackgroundFlush, FlushMemTableToOutputFile, the job-running branch of
BackgroundCompaction, MaybeScheduleFlushOrCompaction) and the remote compaction driver; `PerKeyPointLockManager` (not
ported); EVENT_LOG JSON; where tickers are incremented;
`MirrorFileSystem`; the LOUDS trie bitvector / iterator; most of the `ldb` / `sst_dump` tools beyond the commands
fixed above; `LazyWideColumns*`; small option files (SliceTransform, SstPartitioner, OffpeakTimeOption,
AttributeGroups). Unproven suspicions left by the auditors are listed in the pull request of this audit.
